Use when the user asks to plot, visualize, view, or browse data with fastplotlib or fpl; mentions Figure, Subplot, NDWidget, LineCollection, LineStack, ScatterCollection, ImageGraphic, add_image, add_line, add_scatter, add_nd_image or add_nd_timeseries; wants an interactive viewer, a movie or video player, sliders to scroll through an n-dimensional array, a heatmap, a spike raster, calcium imaging traces, pose tracking, an ethogram, or a spectrogram; wants several datasets synchronized on one time axis; or asks about GPU plottin… ([fastplotlib/claude-skills](https://github.com/fastplotlib/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fastplotlib/skills/fastplotlib) · ★1)
- **fastq-adapter-and-quality-trimming**: Use when raw Illumina short-read FASTQ (single-end or paired-end, DNA-seq or RNA-seq) must be quality-filtered, adapter-trimmed, and QC-reported before alignment or genotyping in a pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-adapter-and-quality-trimming))
- **fastq-adapter-quality-trimming**: Use when raw cfMeDIP-seq (or MeDIP-seq) FASTQ files enter a pipeline and need adapter/quality trimming plus QC, especially when the library may or may not carry UMI or spike-in sequences configured via a YAML file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-adapter-quality-trimming))
- **fastq-adapter-sequence-auto-detection**: Use when trimming Illumina short-read FASTQ data without knowing the adapter sequence, and you need to recover or reimplement fastp's seed-and-nucleotide-tree adapter detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-adapter-sequence-auto-detection))
- **fastq-adaptor-trimming**: Use when raw paired-end FASTQ metatranscriptome reads contain adaptor contamination or low-quality bases and must be trimmed and mate-joined before annotation, e.g. preparing 100 bp paired-end stool RNA-seq files for MG-RAST submission. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-adaptor-trimming))
- **fastq-analysis**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/fastq-analysis) · ★745)
- **fastq-analysis-pipeline**: Guide through omicverse's alignment module for SRA downloading, FASTQ quality control, STAR alignment, gene quantification, and single-cell kallisto/bustools pipelines covering both bulk and single-cell RNA-seq workflows. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fastq-analysis) · ★3.1k · 13 copies)
- **fastq-barcode-extraction-mismatch-tolerant**: Use when sci-L3 combinatorial-indexing FASTQ reads must be demultiplexed into single-cell barcode identities: orienting reads on the RT primer GGGATGCAGCTCGCTCCTG and matching 8-nt Tn5 and 7-nt ligation barcodes with 1-mismatch tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fastq-barcode-extraction-mismatch-tolerant))
- **fastq-base-count-accounting**: Use when you need a per-dataset base-count denominator (e.g., FASTQ datasets like snail, banana, hs1, hs4) to normalize compressed stream sizes into bits-per-base, or to reconcile stream contributions against total compressed size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-base-count-accounting))
- **fastq-dataset-generation**: Use when controlled synthetic FASTQ inputs are needed to benchmark bisulfite aligners under known error rates, and ground-truth read counts and lengths must be exactly reproducible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-dataset-generation))
- **fastq-demultiplexed-sample-processing**: Use when MiniBar (-F binning) has produced per-sample FASTQ files of MinION rDNA amplicon reads and they must be cleaned of low-quality, short reads before consensus/barcode analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-demultiplexed-sample-processing))
- **fastq-downsampling-depth-analysis**: Use when quantifying how isoform-quantification accuracy degrades with sequencing depth: you have simulated Nanopore FASTQ reads with 10x-style cell barcode/UMI, a ground-truth cell-by-isoform matrix, and need depth-response curves of per-cell accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-downsampling-depth-analysis))
- **fastq-duplication-rate-evaluation**: Use when estimating or comparing duplication rates on high-depth paired-end FASTQ data (e.g. ~10000x cfDNA), or when deciding whether FASTQC's per-mate duplication output overestimates the true PE duplication level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-duplication-rate-evaluation))
- **fastq-fasta-read-sampling**: Use when a MinION nanopore read set in FASTA or FASTQ format needs a trained LAST scoring scheme (substitution matrix / parameters) fitted to the specific target before mapping with lastal, instead of relying on default or generic scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-fasta-read-sampling))
- **fastq-file-format-processing**: Use when raw Oxford Nanopore FASTQ reads must have fixed-length primer and adapter sequences stripped from both ends before downstream taxonomic classification, filtering, or de novo/reference-guided assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-file-format-processing))
- **fastq-header-sequence-extraction**: Use when you need to run or reproduce a minimal Watchdog workflow that extracts read headers and sequence subsets from compressed FASTQ files, or when benchmarking Watchdog's resource footprint on a small test dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-header-sequence-extraction))
- **fastq-mate-singleton-conservation-check**: Compare ordered FASTQ mate identities and eligible read conservation against source name/category inventories and explicit singleton and catch-all policies. Use when paired exports have equal counts but wrong pairing, orphan reads disappear or BAM-to-FASTQ recovery is questioned. Not for alignment, biological interpretation or reconstructing unavailable mates or clipped bases. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinformatics/skills/fastq-mate-singleton-conservation-check))
- **fastq-naming-schema-validation**: Use when raw GEX/ADT FASTQ files must be staged before a gExcite/cellranger run and you need to confirm the per-sample directory layout and SAMPLENAME_S[Number]_L00[Lane]_[ReadType]_001.fastq.gz naming before execution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-naming-schema-validation))
- **fastq-paired-end-data-handling**: Use when processing paired-end Illumina FASTQ data (e.g. in.R1.fq.gz/in.R2.fq.gz) and you need duplication-rate evaluation or to replicate fastp's key1/key2 array-based duplication estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-paired-end-data-handling))
- **fastq-paired-end-read-processing**: Use when raw paired-end ChIA-PET FASTQ files (e.g. bridge-linker protocol data such as GSE72816/GSM970213) must be stripped of the 5' bridge linker before read alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-paired-end-read-processing))
- **fastq-preprocessing-and-downsampling**: Use when starting from published scRNA-seq resources (e.g. Cell Ranger 1.1.0 BAMs from Chromium, or oversized FASTQs from ICELL8) that must be converted, reduced to chromosome 21, or downsampled to a fixed read count before pipeline benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-preprocessing-and-downsampling))
- **fastq-preprocessing-benchmarking**: Use when the goal is to quantify how fast and how well FASTQ preprocessing tools (fastp vs Trimmomatic, Cutadapt, AfterQC, SOAPnuke, FASTQC, umis, UMI-tools) perform on Illumina paired-end FASTQ data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-preprocessing-benchmarking))
- **fastq-quality**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/fastq-quality) · ★745)
- **fastq-quality**: Work with FASTQ quality scores using Biopython. Use when analyzing read quality, filtering by quality, trimming low-quality bases, or generating quality reports. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/sequence-io-fastq-quality) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **fastq-quality**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Sequence_Analysis/sequence-io/fastq-quality) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **fastq-quality-assessment**: Use when raw paired-end FASTQ files (e.g. SRA accessions SRP058295, SRP255211) must be QC-checked before STAR alignment or variant calling, or when read origin needs verification via sampled BLAST. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-quality-assessment))
- **fastq-quality-assessment-with-fastqc**: Use when immediately after FASTQ file acquisition (whether from SRA download or local ingestion) and before any trimming or alignment steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fastq-quality-assessment-with-fastqc))
- **fastq-quality-control**: Use when raw FASTQ files (e.g. truncated H3K4me3 ChIPseq or RNAseq reads from mouse embryonic stem cells) need pre-alignment quality assessment with per-cycle quality, base composition, k-mer and read-length reports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-quality-control))
- **fastq-quality-control**: Use when raw FASTQ or FASTA reads (e.g. SRA downloads such as SRR891275) are the starting point of a WGS/WES variant-calling workflow and per-read QC must precede trimming and alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-control))
- **fastq-quality-control**: Use when raw paired-end RNA-seq/metatranscriptome FASTQ files must be cleaned before annotation: mates need merging, low-quality bases and adapters removed, and rRNA reads filtered to speed up annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-control))
- **fastq-quality-control**: Use when paired-end FASTQ files have just been simulated or received (e.g. SimuSCoP output from the eduomics DNA branch) and quality must be assessed before variant calling or other downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-control))
- **fastq-quality-control-reporting**: Use when Illumina (NovaSeq/MGI) FASTQ reads, especially paired-end, need QC profiling plus cleaning — adapter trimming, quality filtering, duplication-rate estimation — before downstream alignment or variant calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-control-reporting))
- **fastq-quality-filtering-and-trimming**: Use when raw paired-end 16S amplicon FASTQ files must be quality-screened (Q30) and length-uniformly prepared before a denoising step such as Deblur that expects short fragments of consistent length. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-filtering-and-trimming))
- **fastq-quality-filtering-per-sample**: Use when raw Oxford Nanopore reads (FASTQ, or FAST5/POD5 requiring basecalling) must be quality-filtered per sample before taxonomic classification or genome reconstruction, and per-sample read-quality statistics are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-filtering-per-sample))
- **fastq-quality-gptomics-bioskills**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/fastq-quality-gptomics-bioskills) · ★745)
- **fastq-quality-metrics-computation**: Use when short-read sequencing data are supplied as FASTQ files and per-cycle/per-read quality summaries must be computed and visually inspected, especially across many files in one report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-quality-metrics-computation))
- **fastq-quality-scores**: Access, filter, and trim Phred quality scores in FASTQ reads using Biopython 1.83+ — including per-position profiles and encoding auto-detection. ([pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills](https://github.com/pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioinformatics-sequence/fastq-quality-scores) · ★8)
- **fastq-quality-summary-parsing**: Use when raw paired-end Illumina FASTQ reads need DADA2/Deblur truncation lengths derived from per-base quality profiles rather than manual inspection or fixed defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-summary-parsing))
- **fastq-quality-trimming**: Use when shotgun metagenomic FASTQs (host-filtered paired and singleton .fastq.gz) must be quality-trimmed before read mapping, and low-quality bases at read ends and overly short reads need removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-trimming))
- **fastq-quality-trimming-phred-threshold**: Use when FASTQ reads with deteriorating 3'-end quality will be used for base-level correction or polishing (e.g. Pilon with Illumina data) and raw per-base qualities must be filtered to a Phred threshold first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-quality-trimming-phred-threshold))
- **fastq-read-alignment-quality-assessment**: Use when starting from raw ChIP-seq or DNase-seq FASTQ (treatment and control) and you need aligned BAMs plus early QC metrics (read quality, GC content, library complexity PBC, fragment size) before peak calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-read-alignment-quality-assessment))
- **fastq-read-barcode-annotation**: Use when FASTQ reads must be filtered for reads containing a known sequence/variant (with mismatches tolerated) and then annotated with cell-barcode and UMI assignments in a single command-line pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-read-barcode-annotation))
- **fastq-read-categorization**: Use when raw WGS FASTQ reads must be reduced to only those overlapping an assembled transcriptome (exonic/flanking content) before expensive k-mer or graph computations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-categorization))
- **fastq-read-count-summary-statistics**: Use when you have run a MetaLAFFA QC step (e.g., Bowtie 2 host filtering against the hs37d5 reference) and need per-sample read counts plus average read length and base-quality summaries to document how many reads were removed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-count-summary-statistics))
- **fastq-read-length-distribution-modeling**: Use when simulating ONT-like sequencing datasets to benchmark mutation detection, requiring FASTQ read-length and base-quality distributions (e.g. 1020–3968 bp, Q10–Q42) matched to the study's Methods parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-length-distribution-modeling))
- **fastq-read-manipulation**: Use when hybrid-correcting noisy PacBio or Oxford Nanopore long reads against aligned Illumina short reads and a questionable position shows ≥90% (η = 0.9) short-read consensus, so the base can be directly replaced without running the optimization-based correction step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-manipulation))
- **fastq-read-output-generation**: Use when the input is raw nanopore FAST5 signal reads and the goal is base-calling each read into a nucleotide sequence collected in a single FASTQ file, without prior signal segmentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fastq-read-output-generation))
- **fastq-read-pair-handling**: Use when reconstructing StrainXpress's read-overlap scoring: you have an interleaved FASTQ of Illumina-type short reads and Minimap2 all-vs-all alignments with CIGAR strings, and need per-pair scores D with &gt;=30 bp / &gt;=0.9 identity filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-pair-handling))
- **fastq-read-pair-processing**: Use when input is paired-end Illumina FASTQ (or CASAVA/QSEQ) reads whose forward and reverse mates overlap an amplicon (e.g. 16S rRNA hypervariable regions) and reads are being discarded (~40%) due to uncalled or miscalled bases under naive perfect-match merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-pair-processing))
- **fastq-read-parsing**: Use when single-end FASTQ data must be parsed to detect adapter sequences automatically (no adapter given as input), or when reproducing fastp's k-mer seed plus nucleotide-tree adapter-detection algorithm from raw read files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-read-parsing))
- **fastq-read-preprocessing-and-counting**: Use when raw paired-end SPLiT-seq FASTQ files need barcode/UMI identification and you must count identified labeled reads and compute identified-read ratios per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-read-preprocessing-and-counting))
- **fastq-read-quality-metrics**: Use when basecalled FASTQ reads from ONT basecallers (e.g., Chiron beam-search vs Albacore) must be compared by error type—insertions, deletions, mismatches—per species, rather than by overall read identity alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-quality-metrics))
- **fastq-read-region-indexing**: Use when preprocessing single-cell genomics FASTQs (e.g. dogmaseq-dig RNA/tag/atac/protein splits) and you need the per-tool technology string describing where barcodes, UMIs, and cDNA lie in R1/R2/R3 before kb count or alevin-fry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-read-region-indexing))
- **fastq-read-sequence-collapsing**: Use when a small RNA-seq FASTQ(.gz) file must be reduced to unique sequences with per-sequence counts before alignment and counting, e.g. producing collapsed.fa as consumed by tiny-count in the tinyRNA pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-read-sequence-collapsing))
- **fastq-read-subset-management**: Use when you have a small FASTA/FASTQ seed read set (e.g. cas-gene or CRISPR-repeat matches) and a large raw FASTQ dataset, and you need to recruit neighboring reads to prime targeted assembly without assembling the whole dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-read-subset-management))
- **fastq-record-length-and-phred-thresholding**: Use when raw pooled/subsampled ONT long-read FASTQs (e.g. .fastq.gz from a MinION run) must be quality-trimmed before plasmid assembly, and every retained record must meet a length and Q-score cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-record-length-and-phred-thresholding))
- **fastq-sample-folder-organization**: Use when preparing raw 10x-style GEX and ADT (CITE-Seq) FASTQs for the gExcite or scAmpi Snakemake pipelines, which require one sub-directory per sample and SAMPLENAME_S[Number]_L00[Lane]_[ReadType]_001.fastq.gz naming before cellranger count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fastq-sample-folder-organization))
- **fastq-sequence-concatenation**: Use when paired-end amplicon reads have been merged per sample (e.g. temp/Sample1.merged.fq) and a uniform filtering pass (primer strip + max expected error) must be applied across all samples in one vsearch call before OTU/ASV analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fastq-sequence-concatenation))
- **fastq-sequence-parsing**: Use when input is a nanopore FASTQ file (possibly gzipped) and the task requires reading or extracting raw base sequences — e.g. for k-mer extraction, MinHash sketching, or read-sequence compression benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-sequence-parsing))
- **fastq-to-alignment-workflow-staging**: Use when deploying the JWES FASTQ-to-alignment pipeline on a Slurm-managed HPC and a job script must be auto-generated from user inputs (project name, FASTQ paths, node count) with '#SBATCH' directives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fastq-to-alignment-workflow-staging))
- **fastq-to-tfrecord-conversion**: Use when feeding FASTQ or FASTA metagenomic reads (e.g. simulated gut-MAG reads or iHMP gut metagenomes) into DeepMicrobes for training or prediction, since the LSTM/embedding models only consume TFRecord input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fastq-to-tfrecord-conversion))
